317

Corporate Social Responsibility Report
Guaranty Trust Holding Company and Subsidiary Companies
CORPORATE SOCIAL RESPONSIBILITY REPORT
Enriching Lives, Creating Shared Value
At GTCO, corporate social responsibility is an essential component of value creation; more than
a corporate obligation, it is central to how we create sustainable value for our stakeholders and
the communities we serve. Guided by our philosophy of enriching lives through CSR, we invest in
initiatives that create opportunities, strengthen communities, and contribute to inclusive
economic development.
Our approach focuses on empowering people, supporting enterprise, advancing inclusion, and
expanding access to opportunities that improve lives. Resilient communities are the foundation
of resilient economies, making every investment in people an investment in long-term shared
prosperity.
During the first half of 2026, our CSR initiatives delivered meaningful social and economic impact
by promoting entrepreneurship, enhancing access to education and healthcare, fostering
innovation, and supporting community development. Through these efforts, we remain
committed to this philosophy while creating lasting value for society and our stakeholders.
2026 GTCO Food and Drink Festival
Everything Food and Drink
The 9th edition of the GTCO Food and Drink Festival, held under the theme Everything Food and
Drink, brought together entrepreneurs, culinary professionals, and consumers on a platform
designed to promote enterprise, celebrate culture, and unlock new opportunities for growth.
The Festival is one of GTCO's flagship enterprise development initiatives, reflecting the Group's
broader commitment to empowering small businesses as a foundation for sustainable economic
growth. Through strategic investments that expand access to markets, strengthen
entrepreneurial capacity, and encourage innovation, GTCO continues to create shared value for
businesses, communities, and the wider economy.
This year's edition provided 204 complimentary retail stalls for SMEs and indigenous food
businesses, complemented by 12 masterclasses facilitated by international chefs and industry
experts. By removing barriers to market access while delivering practical business and technical
knowledge, the Festival enabled entrepreneurs to increase their visibility, strengthen their
capabilities, build valuable networks, and position their businesses for long-term success.
Beyond the numbers, the Festival continues to demonstrate how investing in small businesses
creates broader economic and social value – supporting entrepreneurship, encouraging
innovation, and celebrating Nigeria's rich culinary heritage. In doing so, it contributes to a more
resilient SME ecosystem and inclusive national development.
318

Corporate Social Responsibility Report
Guaranty Trust Holding Company and Subsidiary Companies
Take on Squad 3.0
Smart Systems: The Intelligent Economy
Innovation thrives when young people are given opportunities to solve real world challenges.
Guided by this belief, GTCO, through its payment fintech subsidiary HabariPay, hosted Take on
Squad 3.0, creating a platform where some of Nigeria's brightest tertiary institution students
could transform ideas into practical technology solutions while developing skills relevant to the
digital economy.
Themed Smart Systems: The Intelligent Economy, the hackathon attracted more than 1,600
applications from universities and tertiary institutions across the country, with 600 participants
selected to take part. Working in teams, participants designed and developed digital solutions
using Squad's APIs while benefiting from hands-on mentorship in software development, product
design, user experience, business strategy, and solution scalability.
By combining experiential learning with industry mentorship, the programme strengthened
participants' technical capabilities, problem-solving skills, and entrepreneurial mindset. It also
expanded their exposure to emerging financial technologies and digital innovation, equipping
them with the practical knowledge, professional networks, and confidence required to develop
solutions that address evolving economic and social needs. Team Block X emerged as the overall
winner, while Team SETLD and Team SuperMart secured second and third place, respectively.
Through initiatives such as Take on Squad, GTCO continues to bridge the gap between academic
learning and practical industry experience, helping to develop the next generation of innovators,
entrepreneurs, and technology leaders. By investing in digital talent and innovation-driven
problem-solving, the Group is contributing to the growth of a more inclusive, competitive, and
technology-enabled economy.
NPA Lagos International Polo Tournament
Where Sport Meets Shared Prosperity
Sport is a powerful catalyst for community development, cultural preservation, and economic
growth, and GTCO continues to support platforms that unite people, inspire excellence, and
stimulate enterprise. Through this, the Group creates shared value that extends well beyond the
field of play.
As the main sponsor of the 2026 NPA Lagos International Polo Tournament, GTCO reinforced its
long-standing commitment to one of Africa's premier polo events. The tournament brought
together elite polo teams, spectators, businesses, and visitors from across Nigeria and beyond,
with competitions for the Majekodunmi Cup, Independence Cup, Open Cup, Silver Cup, and Low
Cup, complemented by lifestyle experiences and hospitality offerings that enhanced community
participation.
The tournament's impact extended well beyond the competition itself. By attracting a diverse
audience and creating opportunities for businesses across the hospitality, tourism, retail, and
creative sectors, it generated economic activity while strengthening local enterprise. It also
promoted cultural exchange, encouraged community engagement, and showcased Nigeria as a
destination for international sporting events.
319

Corporate Social Responsibility Report
Guaranty Trust Holding Company and Subsidiary Companies
This continued partnership with the NPA Lagos International Polo Tournament demonstrates
how strategic investment in sport can foster social cohesion, stimulate local economies, and
contribute to long-term national development — reinforcing GTCO's commitment to
strengthening communities and preserving cultural heritage through sustainable economic
opportunity.
Beat the Distance 2.0
Bridging Distances, Expanding Opportunities
Education is a powerful driver of social and economic development, yet for many children in rural
communities, long and difficult journeys to school remain a significant barrier to learning.
Through Beat the Distance 2.0, GTCO continued its commitment to improving access to education
by helping to reduce the distance between children and the opportunities that education
provides.
Building on the success of its inaugural edition in 2019, during which 400 bicycles were
distributed to schoolchildren across rural communities in Enugu, Ondo, and Taraba States, the
programme significantly expanded its reach in 2026. A total of 3,000 bicycles were provided to
schoolchildren in rural communities across Jigawa, Ekiti, Ebonyi, Oyo, Akwa Ibom, and Benue
States, extending the initiative's impact to more underserved communities.
By providing a practical solution to the challenge of long daily commutes, Beat the Distance 2.0
improved access to education by supporting better school attendance, punctuality, and
classroom participation. The initiative also reduced a key barrier to learning, enabling more
children to spend less time travelling and more time in school, while promoting educational
inclusion and equal opportunities for young learners.
By investing in initiatives that improve access to education, GTCO continues to strengthen human
capital development and create lasting social value. Beat the Distance 2.0 contributes to building
stronger communities by empowering children with greater opportunities to learn, thrive, and
realise their full potential, laying the foundation for sustainable social and economic
development.
16 Years of the the Orange Ribbon Initiatives
Acceptance in Action: From Family to Classroom to Workplace to Public Spaces
For 16 years, GTCO's Orange Ribbon Initiative has championed autism awareness, advanced
inclusion, and expanded access to developmental support. Through sustained advocacy and
strategic partnerships, the Initiative continues to foster greater understanding of autism while
supporting individuals on the autism spectrum, their families, caregivers, educators, healthcare
professionals, employers, policymakers, and other stakeholders who play a vital role in creating
a more inclusive society.
As part of this enduring commitment, GTCO hosted its two-day Autism Conference and four-day
Free Consultation Programme under the theme "Acceptance in Action: From Family to Classroom
to Workplace to Public Spaces." The programme brought together families, caregivers,
320

Corporate Social Responsibility Report
Guaranty Trust Holding Company and Subsidiary Companies
educators, healthcare professionals, employers, policymakers, and other stakeholders to explore
practical approaches to creating more inclusive environments across homes, schools,
workplaces, and public spaces.
The two-day conference featured interactive workshops, panel discussions, and practical
sessions led by respected local and international experts in developmental and behavioural
sciences. Over the course of four days, families in both Nigeria and Ghana also benefited from
free one-on-one consultations with medical and developmental specialists. By combining expert
knowledge with direct access to professional guidance, the programme strengthened the
capacity of caregivers and professionals, promoted early identification and intervention, and
equipped communities with practical tools to better support individuals on the autism spectrum.
This sustained investment in awareness and capacity building continues to drive meaningful
social impact by expanding access to developmental support, promoting inclusive practices, and
strengthening collaboration across families, schools, healthcare systems, workplaces, and
communities. Now in its sixteenth year, the Orange Ribbon Initiative is helping to create a more
inclusive society where individuals on the autism spectrum have greater opportunities to
participate, contribute, and thrive.
Bridging the Digital Divide 1.0
Where Technology Meets the Future
Access to digital tools remained a critical barrier for many students preparing for computer-based
examinations across Nigeria. Through Bridging the Digital Divide 1.0, GTCO addressed this gap by
distributing computers to CBT (Computer-Based Test) centres offering free training to students
in underserved communities, helping to strengthen their capacity to deliver quality, hands-on
preparation for digital examinations.
In its maiden edition, the initiative set out to distribute a total of 1,900 computers to CBT centres
across different parts of Nigeria, with deployment carried out in tranches to ensure structured
and sustainable rollout. An initial tranche of 150 computers was distributed to CBT centres in
Kano, Kaduna, and Oyo States, with subsequent tranches planned to extend the initiative's reach
to additional states across the country. The initiative was designed to directly support JAMB and
other examination candidates who have historically faced challenges accessing functional
computers ahead of their exams, helping to level the playing field for students regardless of their
economic or geographic circumstances.
By equipping CBT centres with the computers needed to deliver their free training programmes,
GTCO helped expand access to practical, hands-on preparation for computer-based
examinations. This support enabled more students to build familiarity with digital examination
formats, reducing the anxiety and unfamiliarity that often affect performance on exam day.
As GTCO's newest digital inclusion initiative, Bridging the Digital Divide 1.0 reflected the Group's
continued commitment to expanding access to technology and education. Through this
investment, GTCO helped build a more digitally inclusive Nigeria, where every student has a fair
opportunity to succeed in an increasingly digital examination landscape.
321

Corporate Social Responsibility Report
Guaranty Trust Holding Company and Subsidiary Companies
IN CLOSING
Across classrooms, marketplaces, community centers, healthcare facilities, and sports arenas,
GTCO continues to champion positive change—empowering individuals and communities to
dream bigger, achieve more, and create lasting prosperity. Looking ahead, we remain committed
to expanding our reach, deepening our impact, and fostering a shared future where business and
society grow together.
322
